FROM "Cardapios" AS "c"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT "c0"."CardapiosId", "c0"."PratosId", "p"."Id", "p"."Ativo", "p"."Descricao", "p"."Nome", "p"."Preco", "p"."Tipo", "p"."UsuarioCreateData", "p"."UsuarioCreateId", "p"."UsuarioUpdateData", "p"."UsuarioUpdateId"

    INNER JOIN "Prato" AS "p" ON "c0"."PratosId" = "p"."Id"
) AS "t" ON "c"."Id" = "t"."CardapiosId"

    SELECT "c0"."CardapiosId", "c0"."PratosId", "p"."Id", "p"."Ativo", "p"."Descricao", "p"."Nome", "p"."Preco", "p"."Tipo", "p"."UsuarioCreateData", "p"."UsuarioCreateId", "p"."UsuarioUpdateData", "p"."UsuarioUpdateId"
    FROM "CardapioPrato" AS "c0"
    INNER JOIN "Prato" AS "p" ON "c0"."PratosId" = "p"."Id"
) AS "t" ON "c"."Id" = "t"."CardapiosId"
ORDER BY "c"."Id", "t"."CardapiosId", "t"."PratosId"
